### Implementation and exact local qualification

BuyV2VerticalScrollIndicator follows actual vertical ScrollMetrics with a 2dp silver track and 10dp round rich-blue ball in an 8dp right gutter. ### Latest founder request: assess tools, filters and public user settings

Completed and queued under existing020-R5-A, with related035-R5-A selected-state repair; no additional ticket number and no filter runtime edit. Exact assessment external SHOP-WHOLESALE-FILTERS-AND-PUBLIC-SETTINGS-ASSESSMENT-V1.md,10235bytes/SHA700396D1A879B275B942303B272A09592CD5475E306CBD79DB7BA8C47D40CB12, is linked from child020 and FOUNDER-BUY-UAT-INSTRUCTION-LEDGER-20260907.md.

Source-confirmed findings: Shop Fast/Today/Quick local share the same predicate; Scheduled and Standard/courier duplicate the top mode. Wholesale/Bulk currently split minimumOrder at2, making Flexible MOQ duplicate/conflict with the top choice; bulk freight is a different concept. Lowest delivered/landed price checks badge text instead of delivered cost; nearby/delivery deadlines use city-name heuristics. selectedFilter and selectedFulfilmentMode can disagree with the displayed Any state; changing the top mode leaves a conflicting fulfilment mode, and Clear does not clear selectedFilter. Delivery is repeated in filters, refinement and Shopping settings. Refinements apply immediately despite the separate Show action; facets/counts inherit10-brand/18-product limits. These findings need explicit Redmi reproduction and coherent repair, not a source-only acceptance claim.

Required repair direction: one compact existing Sort and filter sheet opened directly, scoped to destination/mode/category/search/store. Keep top purchase modes as the single mode selector. Shop prioritises price, brand, pack size and availability; Wholesale adds explicit MOQ/quantity and supported freight/source facts. Keep Sort separate; remove duplicate predicates and misleading unbacked promises. Use one selected-state model, draft Show/Cancel/Clear behaviour, truthful scoped counts/facets and explicit browse retention. Clear preserves mode/category/query/address/cart. Arrival deadlines, landed totals and serviceability need authoritative data; backend next phase remains explicit. Global Profile owns accessibility/language/privacy/security/account notification preferences; reuse address/payment owners with contextual checkout shortcuts. Remove browsing Delivery filter from account settings, while preserving all existing tools and exact Back routes. No duplicate shared Profile or retailer screen.

Next: atomic source/test/results checkpoint under the established gates; resume guarded Redmi observation at313, complete remaining original/child and expanded action enumeration, then grouped repairs including020/035. ## Founder addition: R66-UAT-SCROLL-001 — thin vertical track with a round ball

Registered on 2026-09-06 from the founder's attached scrollbar reference, then explicitly clarified: a circular ball travels up and down a thin vertical track. Status: queued frontend enhancement, linked to R66-UAT-SCALE-001. Apply this vertical design to applicable vertically scrollable category/product pages across Shop, Wholesale, Offers and Store catalogues. The ball must be circular, with equal width and height, rather than an elongated handle. Use the established rich blue for the ball; rich green is an allowed context-appropriate alternative. Use a silver track on light surfaces or a white track where the background provides clear contrast. The clarification supersedes the earlier horizontal/vertical interpretation; horizontal rails retain their existing behavior under this ticket.

Acceptance: show the vertical indicator when its content actually overflows; keep it visibly discoverable while browsing; move the ball up or down from the actual vertical scroll position and available extent; avoid covering text, product imagery, quantity/Cart controls or navigation. Preserve native touch scrolling and accessibility targets/announcements. If ball dragging is supported, provide a usable touch area around the small circle and verify that it does not steal adjacent category/product taps. Check that horizontal rail gestures do not move the vertical ball unless the page actually scrolls vertically. Test top/bottom, overflow appearing/disappearing, search/category/location changes, restoration after product/Store/Cart/Back, portrait/landscape and enlarged text. Preserve the compact layout without adding a tall strip. Local functional/actual visual checks precede retained-data Redmi verification.

Pagination linkage: regional discovery and nationally serviceable search retain the correct provider/category scope, with up to 5,000 SKUs per store across 100,000 providers. Do not imply that the currently loaded page window is the entire inventory or that an unknown total has been reached. Bind indicator state to the paged source and its query generation; only offer global position/seek when total and range navigation are supported. Backend cursor/search/serviceability work remains next phase. This is an additional founder enhancement ticket, not a new confirmed Redmi defect. Detailed local ticket: apps/mobile/build/r66-3-prebuild-20260906/TICKET-R66-UAT-SCROLL-001.md.

## Founder addition: R66-UAT-SCALE-001 — provider and SKU pagination

Founder authorizes persistent development test data intended for later backend connection. Use stable provider/SKU/category IDs, explicit regional and national serviceability, inventory/offer relationships and a versioned reproducible dataset manifest. Keep provider identity separate from display names. Consume fixtures through the frontend data-source boundary and document field/ID mapping so a later backend adapter can replace the fixture source while preserving pagination and navigation tests. This is development data, not a claim that live provider inventory or backend integration already exists. Seed/generate pages on demand with stable IDs; preserve edited Cart/Saved state through page eviction. Backend schema/API mapping must be reconciled with the authoritative backend contract in the next phase.

Founder discovery scope clarification: default screen availability is regional; search also supports nationally available vendors that can serve the selected delivery location. Do not display the entire provider estate as the default feed. Bind each continuation to delivery region/location, regional or national search scope, query, category, destination, sort and selected provider; invalidate obsolete responses when any scope changes. Preserve the correct scope and scroll anchor on product/Store/Cart/Back. Use explicit serviceability fixtures for frontend acceptance, including a national vendor that can serve the location and one that cannot, empty regional results, national search results and location changes mid-request. Do not invent serviceability or assert that every national vendor serves every region. The authoritative geographic/serviceability/search contract remains a next-phase backend dependency.

Founder clarification: 5,000 SKUs are listed per individual store, across an estate of 100,000 vendors/providers. Store and category pagination must expose every matching SKU through all required pages, not stop at a fixed preview or catalogue cap. At a chosen page size P, an unfiltered 5,000-SKU store needs ceil(5,000/P) reachable pages (for example, 100 pages if P=50; page size remains a measured implementation choice). Category results page through their complete matching subset and reset/restore independently by store and category. Provider discovery separately pages through up to 100,000 providers. Cross-provider Shop, Wholesale, Offers and category discovery must retain vendor identity and its own query-scoped continuation. Frontend tests must reach the last SKU and last provider using generated paged data, verify category completeness and backtracking, and keep memory bounded; this does not require loading every store's inventory at once.

Status: registered; frontend source assessment complete; implementation and local/Redmi acceptance pending. This is a founder-requested capacity ticket added on 2026-09-06, separate from the35 original UAT tickets, nine R2 children and ten confirmed R4 children. Keep their counts unchanged. Target:100,000 providers with up to5,000 SKUs per provider (up to500,000,000 provider-SKU listings in the service estate), across Shop, Wholesale, Offers and category results, including each provider's Store/full catalogue. This target is not a claim that the phone should hold all listings or that production capacity has been validated.

Frontend evidence: buy_v2_catalogue.dart uses an8-item progressive reveal and lazy ListView.separated item builders, but accepts a complete List<BuyV2Product>, copies every incoming product ID on widget updates and grows the revealed prefix. buy_v2_session.dart filters/ranks/sorts the local catalogue; visibleProducts has an18-item cap for an unsearched all-category result, and partnerCatalogueFor defaults to50 with no continuation returned. The Store caller uses that default, then previews six. These are specific frontend scale risks to investigate, not an observed500-million-item production incident. Existing lazy widget construction must be preserved, but is not sufficient evidence of bounded data loading.

Frontend acceptance: injectable paged provider/product source using deterministic generated fixtures; bounded page requests, retained pages, image/cache work and widget creation independent of total inventory; stable provider+SKU identity and cursor/query generation; no duplicate request or duplicate/missing result at page boundaries; discard stale responses after search/category/sort/provider/destination changes; keep Cart/Saved quantities and the exact product/Store/scroll return context. Cover initial loading, next-page loading, retry without losing loaded rows, empty results and truthful end-of-list/count states. Every Shop/Wholesale/Offers/category/Store entry must reach beyond the current small caps. Verify normal/enlarged portrait/landscape visuals locally before a uniquely qualified retained-data Redmi test, including long scrolling, rapid filter changes, retry, Back and Cart continuity. Record measured memory/frame/request/page limits and the fixture generation contract; do not construct the full500-million-row estate in memory or equate fixture results with live backend acceptance.

Backend explicitly remains next phase: provider catalogue ingestion, database/index/search design, global filtering/ranking, cursor API implementation, authoritative counts/availability/pricing, caching/CDN, service throughput and real production load/capacity acceptance. Frontend may define and test the consumer interface with mocks; it must not simulate successful production backend completion. Detailed local ticket: apps/mobile/build/r66-3-prebuild-20260906/TICKET-R66-UAT-SCALE-001.md. R66-UAT-014 remains the original accessibility narration defect; this capacity request does not replace or reopen it by renaming.

## 011-R5-A/B — compact delivery progress and distinct Keep, Hide and sound (7 September 2026)

The existing delivery control now shows a truck and an exact-order progress streak in its compact44dp rail target. Tapping expands the existing panel. Keep pins it open across the45-second idle period and Buy navigation; Kept identifies the selected state. Collapse or Hide releases that pin. Hide removes the compact entry, and Show delivery status on the same order's Tracking/Assist view restores it without changing the order or leaving that view. Holding the panel pauses its collapse timer; releasing resumes the45-second idle period when it is not kept. Tracking/Assist retain their full progress view without a duplicate overlay. This is an mvp_required repair to original011 and children011-R5-A/B under the existing founder-authorized umbrella, not a new destination or delivery service.

Arrival sound is independently off by default. Enabling first prepares an actual short local cue; unavailable preparation leaves it off with compact inline recovery. A genuine foreground transition to arriving or delivered plays at most once per order; hydration, rebuild, Back, resume, repeated status and customer collection do not manufacture alerts. Account/session replacement, route obscuring, backgrounding, disabling and disposal invalidate late work. A failed play turns the choice off with Sound unavailable. Try again. The UI explicitly says Sounds while you use the app. The existing just_audio/path_provider dependencies are reused for a short app-temporary WAV, with cancellation, bounded player waits and cleanup of only the unique cue directory. No dependency, asset manifest, native configuration, device volume or explicit shared audio-session configuration was changed. Physical Redmi sound, focus, interruption and silent-mode behavior remain unqualified; no real audio was played during host tests.
